### [269] [DefoEye: Python-Based Software for Facilitating Time-Series InSAR Analysis of Sentinel-1 Remote-Sensing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04915)
*Alireza Taheri Dehkordi,Hossein Hashemi,Amir Naghibi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: DefoEye(v1)是一个基于Python的开源软件包，封装了GMTSAR，为Sentinel-1数据提供统一、用户友好的时间序列InSAR处理流程，支持并行作业、干涉图网络剪枝和多种锚定选项。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于TS-InSAR（时间序列干涉合成孔径雷达）数据处理软件工具，属于InSAR形变监测领域，不涉及遥感图像目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心问题。虽然使用了SAR数据，但处理的是干涉相位而非目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关。该论文属于InSAR形变监测数据处理领域，不涉及遥感目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：TS-InSAR形变监测数据处理；应用场景：地质、水文、环境形变监测；传感器类型：Sentinel-1 SAR；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型。DefoEye是一个基于Python的软件工具包，封装了GMTSAR，提供统一的工作流。摘要未说明模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"pruning"是指干涉图网络剪枝（interferogram network pruning），属于InSAR数据处理流程中的步骤，而非深度学习模型剪枝。不涉及模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该软件为地面数据处理工具，不涉及星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Sentinel-1数据，覆盖意大利博洛尼亚、瑞典哥特兰、美国休斯顿、伊朗卡拉季四个区域（2020-2024年）；评价指标：RMSE（4.3-11.9 mm）、Pearson相关系数（0.63-0.98）；对比数据：10个GNSS站观测数据。

Experiments: 在四个不同地质、形变机制和气候条件的区域进行了验证。与GNSS观测对比，RMSE为4.3-11.9 mm，Pearson相关系数为0.63-0.95；在无GNSS数据的区域与其他处理工具对比，RMSE为4.8 mm/yr，Pearson相关系数为0.98。

Limitations: 1. 仅支持Sentinel-1数据，地理适用性受限；2. 不涉及目标检测或深度学习模型；3. 无轻量化或星上部署相关信息；4. 作为软件工具，性能依赖于底层GMTSAR。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题相关性极低，直接借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：1) 论文中"干涉图网络剪枝"的概念可类比思考检测模型中的冗余连接剪枝思路；2) 开源软件的设计理念可借鉴到星上检测模型的工程化部署中。但整体而言，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Many existing time-series Interferometric Synthetic Aperture Radar (TS-InSAR) software tools have limitations, including restricted geographic applicability, commercial licensing, and incomplete end-to-end processing support. Although GMTSAR avoids some of these constraints, it still requires substantial manual intervention and C-shell commands, lacks a user-friendly graphical interface, and omits important steps such as interferogram network pruning and anchoring of unwrapped interferograms. This paper introduces DefoEye (v1), an open-source Python-based software package that wraps GMTSAR and provides a unified, user-friendly TS-InSAR workflow for Sentinel-1 data. DefoEye supports parallel job execution, interferogram network pruning, and multiple anchoring options. Its performance was evaluated from 2020 to 2024 in four regions with different geological settings, deformation mechanisms, and atmospheric and climatic conditions. In Bologna, Italy; Gotland, Sweden; and Houston, USA, DefoEye results were compared with observations from 10 GNSS stations and showed strong agreement, with RMSE values of 4.3-11.9 mm and Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.63-0.95. In Karaj, Iran, where GNSS observations were unavailable, DefoEye was compared with other widely used processing tools and achieved similarly close agreement, with an RMSE of 4.8 mm/yr and a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.98. These results demonstrate that DefoEye provides reliable TS-InSAR products for geological, hydrological, and environmental applications.

</details>


### [270] [AI-based single-shot structured-light depth reconstruction for real-time laparoscopic surgical guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05109)
*Wayne Wonseok Rodgers,Xiangyi Le,Seonghoon Jang,Shuwen Wei,Justin Opfermann,Michael Kam,Axel Krieger,Jin U. Kang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LED照明二进制掩模和VQ-VAE先验的无同步单次深度感知方法，用于腹腔镜手术中的深度估计。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于医学内窥镜手术中的深度感知，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化方法（仅报告了在A100 GPU上的26Hz推理速度，未讨论参数量、FLOPs、剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：单次深度估计（depth estimation）；场景：机器人腹腔镜手术（robotic laparoscopic surgery）；传感器：LED照明二进制掩模投影模块+双通道腹腔镜；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，仅重建手术场景深度图。

Model architecture: VQ-VAE编码器将输入映射为离散潜空间表示，潜空间中的U-Net作为深度预测头（depth head），无单独掩码预测分支。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗。仅报告在NVIDIA A100 GPU上达到26.0 Hz（301帧连续推理），未讨论边缘部署或星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：722张配对体模图像（phantom images），使用Zivid 3D相机获取参考深度；评价指标：MAE（3.70 mm）、AbsRel（0.0326）、delta=1.1 accuracy（0.962）、delta=1.1^2 accuracy（0.970）。

Experiments: 与dual U-Net MaskNet+DepthNet基线对比，MAE更低；优于现成的单目深度模型（monocular depth models）在MAE、AbsRel和阈值精度指标上。固定训练/验证/测试集划分。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未讨论任何模型轻量化或边缘部署策略；4. 数据集规模较小（722张），且为医学体模数据，非遥感图像；5. 仅在A100 GPU上测试，未评估在资源受限设备上的性能。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的技术参考是VQ-VAE潜空间表示用于深度估计的思路，但遥感在轨目标检测通常不需要深度估计，且该工作未涉及轻量化部署，借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Significance. Accurate intraoperative depth perception is important for autonomous and semi-autonomous robotic laparoscopic surgery. Conventional fringe projection profilometry can achieve millimeter-scale accuracy but often requires multi-shot acquisition, digital-micromirror-device projection, and projector-camera synchronization, complicating integration into compact laparoscopic systems.
  Aim. To develop a synchronization-free, single-shot depth-sensing platform using a passive LED-illuminated binary mask and a VQ-VAE prior with a custom U-Net depth head.
  Approach. A compact projection module was coupled to one channel of a dual-channel laparoscope, while the second channel imaged the fringe-illuminated target. A Zivid 3D camera acquired reference depth for 722 paired phantom images. Zivid depth maps were reprojected into the SSLE image frame for supervised training and evaluation. The VQ-VAE encoded each input into a discrete latent representation, and a latent-space U-Net predicted depth without a separate mask-prediction branch.
  Results. Using a fixed train/validation/test split, the proposed model achieved an MAE of 3.70 mm, AbsRel of 0.0326, delta=1.1 accuracy of 0.962, and delta=1.1^2 accuracy of 0.970. It achieved lower MAE than the dual U-Net MaskNet + DepthNet baseline and outperformed off-the-shelf monocular depth models in MAE, AbsRel, and threshold accuracy. The pipeline operated at 26.0 Hz over 301 consecutive frames on an NVIDIA A100 GPU.
  Conclusions. The LED-illuminated binary-pattern platform with latent-space depth reconstruction enables synchronization-free, video-rate endoscopic depth estimation. Results demonstrate Zivid-referenced phantom reconstruction without an explicit segmentation stage, while emphasizing the importance of dataset size and SSLE-Zivid calibration accuracy.

</details>

### [271] [Kitchen Robotic Manipulation utilizing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04042)
*Myung-Hwan Jeon,Sankalp Yamsani,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向家庭厨房场景的模块化机器人感知流水线，集成开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、3D重建与6D位姿估计，用于餐具操作任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦家庭机器人操作（厨房餐具处理），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、aerial image等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、6D位姿估计、抓取规划；应用场景：家庭厨房机器人操作（餐具处理）；传感器类型：RGB-D相机；目标类别：餐具（碗、杯子等）。

Model architecture: 模块化流水线，包含：LLMDet（开放词汇目标检测）、SAMv2（分割）、DINOv2（视觉特征）、GeoTransformer（3D几何配准），以及2D-3D特征融合策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及可在物理机器人上部署，但未涉及星上/边缘部署约束。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自定义厨房数据集（20场景，含杂乱和遮挡条件）；评价指标：ADI（Average Distance of Model Points，平均模型点距离），最佳配置达89.12%。

Experiments: 在20场景厨房基准上评估最佳配置（LLMDet+SAMv2+DINOv2+GeoTransformer），ADI达89.12%；真实机器人演示验证了水槽到洗碗机转移、杯子堆叠等任务。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或压缩技术；3. 仅针对室内厨房场景，不具备遥感场景泛化性；4. 未报告计算资源需求。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。模块化流水线设计思路（可替换不同基础模型）在遥感任务中可参考，但整体方向差异过大。开放词汇检测（如LLMDet）在遥感场景中可能有扩展潜力，但摘要未提供轻量化相关技术。

Abstract: Deploying robots in everyday human environments requires perception systems that are both robust and adaptable to diverse, dynamic conditions. In this work, we present a modular perception pipeline for household manipulation tasks, with a focus on dishware handling in kitchen environments. The pipeline integrates open-vocabulary object detection, multi-view segmentation, instance-aware 3D reconstruction, and a 2D-3D feature fusion strategy for 6D pose estimation and grasp planning. Its modular design enables systematic substitution of multiple visual and geometric foundation models, allowing us to identify the best-performing configuration through extensive evaluation on a custom kitchen dataset. The best-performing configuration (LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer) achieves an ADI of 89.12\% on the 20-scene kitchen benchmark with cluttered and occluded conditions. Furthermore, real-world demonstrations confirm that the best configuration can be deployed on physical robots without environment-specific retraining, successfully executing tasks such as sink-to-dishwasher transfer and cup stacking. It validates the adaptability and scalability of the pipeline and highlights its potential as a practical framework for household robotic systems. Our code and supplementary materials are available at https://raivlab.github.io/FM_kitchen .

</details>


### [272] [Interpretable Fuzzy Inference for UAV Target Tracking Using Bounding-Box Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04121)
*Reza Ahmari,Ahmad Mohammadi,Vahid Hemmati,Nicholas Edmond,Hossein Z. Saghazadeh,Olusola Odeyomi,Parham Kebria,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊推理的可解释框架，从YOLO检测框的低维特征（目标质心位置、面积、长宽比）生成连续偏航指令，用于无人机视觉引导，强调数据高效、计算轻量且可解释。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及UAV视觉引导和YOLO目标检测，但场景是无人机对地面车辆的协同导航，非遥感卫星在轨目标检测；轻量化体现在模糊推理系统的低计算开销，而非面向遥感模型的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等主流轻量化方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向弱相关，不涉及遥感场景、不涉及模型压缩/加速核心方法，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：UAV视觉引导UGV的连续偏航角估计；场景：空地协同机器人导航；传感器：机载视觉（VICON运动捕捉环境标注）；目标类别：地面车辆（UGV）。非遥感卫星场景。

Model architecture: 两阶段框架：1) YOLO检测器提取目标框；2) 从检测框提取低维特征（质心位置、面积、长宽比），输入模糊推理系统。模糊系统包括Mamdani基线模型和Takagi-Sugeno模型（每输入3个隶属项，共27条规则）。

Lightweight method: 非传统轻量化方法。轻量性来源于：1) 仅使用YOLO检测框的3个低维特征，避免复杂几何建模；2) 模糊推理系统本身计算开销极低（27条规则）；3) 参数从训练集分位数直接导出，无需迭代优化。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未明确报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅定性说明"computationally lightweight"和"real-time"，但无具体数值。模糊推理系统本身计算量小，适合资源受限平台，但YOLO检测器的开销未讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6,169个标注样本，来自VICON运动捕捉环境。评价指标：MAE (0.140°±0.003°)、RMSE (0.200°±0.008°)、最大绝对误差 (1.254°±0.121°)、阈值内准确率（±1°: 99.676%, ±3°和±5°: 100%）、方向一致性 (90.254%±0.612%)。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 5次随机训练-测试划分的交叉验证。Takagi-Sugeno模型优于Mamdani基线。未与其他深度学习方法或轻量化方法对比。

Limitations: 1) 非遥感/卫星场景，不涉及遥感图像特性（多光谱、SAR、大视场、地物背景复杂等）；2) 不涉及目标检测模型本身的轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）；3) 无参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标；4) 依赖YOLO检测框质量，YOLO模型开销未分析；5) 仅在受控室内环境验证，未在真实遥感或野外场景测试。

Ideas for my research: 1) 模糊推理作为后处理轻量决策模块的思路可借鉴，但需适配遥感场景；2) 从检测框提取低维特征（位置、面积、长宽比）进行后续处理的思想可用于星上轻量后处理；3) 整体而言借鉴价值有限，因为不涉及遥感目标检测模型本身的轻量化核心问题。

Abstract: Vision-based guidance of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) toward unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) supports cooperative aerial--ground robotics, but reliable continuous yaw estimation from onboard vision remains challenging because of sensing uncertainty, limited computation, and the need for interpretable control. Existing deep-learning and geometric-reconstruction approaches often require large datasets, external localization, or complex modeling assumptions, reducing transparency and deployment suitability on resource-constrained platforms. We present an interpretable fuzzy-inference framework that generates continuous yaw commands from low-dimensional features extracted from YOLO boxes: target centroid location, area, and aspect ratio. No explicit geometric modeling is required. A Mamdani fuzzy system serves as an interpretable baseline using a shoulder--triangle--shoulder input partition. It is followed by a first-order Takagi--Sugeno model with three antecedent membership terms per input, whose parameters are derived from training-set quantiles, yielding a compact 27-rule structure. Evaluation uses 6{,}169 labeled samples from a VICON motion-capture environment. Across five randomized train--test splits, the Takagi--Sugeno model achieves a test-set mean absolute error of $0.140^\circ \pm 0.003^\circ$, a root mean squared error of $0.200^\circ \pm 0.008^\circ$, and a maximum absolute error of $1.254^\circ \pm 0.121^\circ$. Within-threshold accuracies are $99.676% \pm 0.270%$ for $\pm1^\circ$ and $100.000% \pm 0.000%$ for both $\pm3^\circ$ and $\pm5^\circ$. Directional consistency between image-plane horizontal displacement and predicted yaw sign reaches $90.254% \pm 0.612%$. These results show that the framework is transparent, data-efficient, computationally lightweight, and suitable for real-time vision-based UAV guidance toward mobile ground targets.

</details>


### [273] [SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04196)
*Nie Lin,Takehiko Ohkawa,Sijin Chen,Ruoshi Wen,Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Zhengming Zhu,Yiming Bao,Yunfei Li,Minjie Cai,Xiao Ma,Wei Xu,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相似度的数据挖掘框架SiMDex，用于从大规模第一视角人类视频中筛选对灵巧操作任务有用的数据，以提升VLA模型的后训练效果。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人灵巧操作（dexterous manipulation）中的人类视频数据筛选，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。属于机器人学与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的交叉方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人类视频数据挖掘与筛选；应用场景：机器人灵巧操作；传感器类型：第一视角（egocentric）摄像头；目标类别：人类操作动作。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: 采用三阶段召回-排序-重排序（recall-ranking-re-ranking）流水线，在形态无关的动作空间（morphology-agnostic action space）中操作，不改变VLA架构或训练流程。具体模型结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：约3200万第一视角人类样本（~32M egocentric human samples）；评价指标：任务成功率（success rate）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 使用约149万挖掘样本（不到数据池的5%），将成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%，表明选择性数据筛选优于随机混合。对比方法为随机采样基线。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文方向不同，但其"数据筛选优于随机混合"的思想在遥感领域的数据增强或难例挖掘中可能有间接启发，但与本课题核心（轻量化模型设计、在轨部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an explosive trend of scaling ego-centric human videos for robot manipulation, yet it remains unclear which data actually benefits dexterous manipulation. We present SiMDex, a similarity-based data mining framework that casts human data selection for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation as a recommendation problem. For each robot demonstration, SiMDex employs a three-layer recall-ranking-re-ranking pipeline to extract task-relevant subsets from a pool of ~32M egocentric human samples, operating in a morphology-agnostic action space that requires no changes to VLA architecture or training. Against a strong baseline trained with an equal amount of randomly sampled human data, SiMDex uses only ~1.49M mined samples (<5% of the pool) yet improves the overall success rate from 47.7% to 61.1%, showing that selective curation outperforms indiscriminate data mixing.

</details>


### [274] [Feasibility of Embedded Photoplethysmography Sensing in Short-Duration Tactile Interactions With Pocket-Sized Robots Using IMU- and Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04242)
*Turjja Datta,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究将PPG传感器嵌入陪伴机器人以实现无接触心率监测，通过IMU滤波消除运动伪影，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。论文主题是儿童陪伴机器人的嵌入式心率监测，属于人机交互与可穿戴传感领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：心率监测（heart rate monitoring）；应用场景：儿童陪伴机器人（companion robot for children）；传感器类型：嵌入式PPG传感器（embedded photoplethysmography sensor）+ IMU；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感卫星目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 两阶段滤波流水线（two-stage filtering pipeline）：第一阶段利用IMU拒绝高方差片段（reject high-variance segments），第二阶段采用基于置信度的平滑算法（confidence-based smoothing algorithm）用于恢复期。无深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。论文涉及的是信号处理滤波算法，而非深度学习模型的剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 涉及嵌入式部署（embedded sensor），但讨论的是PPG传感器嵌入机器人而非深度学习模型部署。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理等指标。与星上/边缘部署深度学习模型无关。

Datasets and metrics: 实验：Within-Subjects Study，26名参与者；对比设备：腕戴式传感器（wrist-worn sensor）；评价指标：Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE)，统计显著性检验（p<0.05）。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 滤波策略显著降低了MAPE，实现了与地面真值测量的统计等价性（p<0.05）。短时交互分析表明传感器需要较长时间的稳定才能收敛。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型架构、轻量化方法或部署策略。论文本身局限包括：需要较长时间稳定才能收敛，运动伪影仍是挑战。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：其IMU辅助滤波的思想在遥感领域并不适用（遥感无运动伪影问题）。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Ubiquitous companion robots offer a promising avenue for immediate anxiety relief in children, yet their effectiveness relies on the ability to monitor physiological states continuously and unobtrusively. Current solutions often depend on external wearables, which impose usability barriers and limit the robot's autonomy. This paper investigates the integration of an embedded photoplethysmography (PPG) sensor directly into a pocket-sized companion robot, AffectaPocket, to enable self-contained heart rate monitoring during tactile interaction. We address the significant challenge of motion artifacts inherent in handheld usage by implementing a two-stage filtering pipeline that utilizes an onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) to reject high-variance segments and a confidence-based smoothing algorithm for recovery periods. We evaluated the system against a commonly used wrist worn sensor in a Within-Subjects Study with 26 participants. Our results demonstrate that the filtering strategy significantly reduced the Mean Absolute Percentage Error and achieved statistical equivalence to the ground truth measurements (p<0.05). Analysis of short-duration interactions shows that the sensor requires stability over longer periods to converge.

</details>


### [275] [SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04246)
*Harshitha Rajaprakash,Aditeya Prajapati,Rong Xue,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAFECAST方法，利用对比集扰动改进隐状态风险探针的训练与校准，用于检测视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时分布偏移下的失败情况。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人操作领域的视觉-语言-动作策略的失败检测与分布偏移鲁棒性，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作策略的失败检测（failure detection）；应用场景：DROID真实机器人环境、LIBERO仿真环境；传感器类型：视觉（RGB）+ 语言指令；目标类别：机器人操作任务中的物体（clutter, distractor objects, novel objects等）。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于VLM（视觉-语言模型）backbone的隐状态风险探针（hidden-state risk probe），结合功能共形预测（functional conformal prediction）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标，且场景为地面机器人而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DROID（真实机器人数据集）、LIBERO（仿真环境）；评价指标：ROC-AUC（失败检测的受试者工作特征曲线下面积）。

Experiments: SAFECAST在多个VLM backbone上相比SOTA基线显著提升了失败检测ROC-AUC；视觉和语言对比集扰动联合使用效果最佳；sim-to-real校准优于仅使用真实rollout数据的探针训练。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作完全面向机器人操作领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但"对比集扰动（contrast set perturbations）增强训练数据以提升分布偏移下检测鲁棒性"的思想，理论上可迁移到遥感在轨目标检测中应对光照变化、视角变化、传感器噪声等分布偏移场景，不过需要大量改造。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often fail under deployment-time distribution shifts such as clutter, distractor objects, lighting changes, novel objects, altered initial states, and reworded instructions. Hidden-state-based risk probes combined with functional conformal prediction can detect rollout failures, but their reliability depends on calibration data matching deployment conditions. We introduce SAFECAST, which leverages contrast set perturbations to improve hidden-state probe training and calibration for deployment time shift. SAFECAST statistically significantly improves failure detection ROC-AUC scores over a state of the art baseline in both real-world DROID and LIBERO simulation experiments across multiple VLM backbones. We further find that SAFECAST benefits most when both visual and language contrast set perturbations are used to augment data, and that with contrast set perturbations, sim-to-real calibration leads to better probes than using real rollout data only.

</details>


### [276] [Structured LLM Reasoning for Zero-Shot Human--Robot Coordination Under Hidden Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04309)
*Dong Hae Mangalindan,Anand Gokhale,Francesco Bullo,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结构化大语言模型架构，用于零样本人机协作建筑任务，通过ToM推理、分层规划、对话理解、动作验证和反馈重规划实现协调。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究人机协作建筑任务中的零样本协调问题，与遥感卫星、地球观测、目标检测、模型轻量化/在轨部署完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人机协作建筑任务（cooperative construction task）；场景：室内/模拟环境；传感器：未说明；目标类别：未说明。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: 结构化LLM架构，包含五个模块：(i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, (v) feedback-based replanning。基于Dec-POMDP框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：人类参与者实验（human-participant experiments）；评价指标：交互步数（interaction steps）、信任评分（trust ratings）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 与两个基线对比：(1) 无ToM推理的消融版本；(2) 离线训练的多智能体强化学习策略。所提方法在更少交互步数和更高信任评分上优于两个基线。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该论文与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，LLM结构化分解决策的思路可能对遥感在轨任务规划有极间接的启发，但与目标检测和轻量化核心问题无关。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model (LLM) architecture for zero-shot human--robot coordination in a cooperative construction task with private goal views. Guided by a Dec-POMDP formulation, the architecture decomposes decision-making into (i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, and (v) feedback-based replanning. We compare the proposed method with an ablation without ToM inference and a multi-agent reinforcement-learning policy trained offline over many goal pairs. In human-participant experiments, the proposed method required fewer interaction steps and yielded higher post-interaction trust ratings than both baselines. These results suggest that systematically decomposing the team decision problem, using LLMs as tractable surrogates for otherwise intractable inference and planning computations, and retaining conventional verification for physical feasibility can improve both task coordination and the human experience.

</details>


### [277] [Design and Flight of an Ion-propelled Micro Hovercraft Leveraging Ground Proximity Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04343)
*C. Luke Nelson,Grant Nations,Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电空气动力学（EAD）推进的微型离子气垫船，利用地面效应提升推力效率，实现了低噪声、长续航的悬浮飞行，但该工作与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是电空气动力学推进的微型气垫船（ion-propelled micro hovercraft），属于机器人/无人机推进系统领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文涉及微型飞行器/气垫船的推进系统设计，不涉及遥感、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等深度学习部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集。评价指标为推力效率（16 mN/W）、自重（1.6 g）、额外载荷（1.5 g）等物理参数。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文进行了不同几何构型和被动气垫裙的实验表征，并制造了实物样机进行飞行测试，但无深度学习相关实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于电空气动力学推进的微型飞行器设计，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Electroaerodynamic propulsion is compelling for use in micro air vehicles due to its silent and solid-state nature, but its limited efficiency has thus far precluded a path towards power-autonomous flight. Recent work has shown that thrust density and efficiency for small-scale atmospheric ion thrusters can be vastly increased when operating close to a ground plane. Here, we explore the design space of centimeter-scale hovercraft, which can leverage this ground effect for low-altitude flight. We first perform an empirical investigation, characterizing the performance benefits and trade-offs for different geometries and configurations of passive hovercraft skirts, then use the results to fabricate a viable point design. We demonstrate a palm-sized hovercraft that, while tethered to an external power source, can fly for extended periods, withstand dozens of takeoff and landing cycles, passively stabilize to reject significant mechanical disturbances, and generate practically zero audible noise signature. The measured thrust efficiency of 16 mN/W and additional payload capacity of almost 1.5 grams above the vehicle's self mass of about 1.6 grams exceeds any similarly sized electroaerodynamically propelled robot by an order of magnitude. This is the first time an ion-propelled micro hovercraft has been shown in the open literature, and our work points the way towards an entirely new class of robot.

</details>


### [278] [Approximate Multi-Objective Search Under Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04398)
*Omar Muhammetkulyyev,Oren Salzman,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出epsilon-rule-dominance概念和RA*pex搜索算法，用于高效计算多目标机器人规划中的规则书近似最优解集。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人运动规划中的多目标优化搜索算法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为机器人多目标规划搜索算法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向无任何直接关联，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多目标机器人运动规划；应用场景：机器人规划（安全、效率、合规性）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: RA*pex：基于最佳优先搜索的多目标规划算法，利用维度缩减、分离的closed sets、截断和残差规则集进行支配检查。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。该论文讨论的是搜索算法的计算效率，而非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集；评价指标为计算时间（computation times），相比现有方法快两个数量级。

Experiments: 实验表明RA*pex的计算时间比现有方法快两个数量级（over two orders of magnitude faster）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。epsilon-rule-dominance的近似支配思想在理论上可类比于模型压缩中的近似精度保持策略，但关联性极低，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Robotic planning often involves multiple objectives with complex priority relationships, such as safety, efficiency, and regulatory compliance. Rulebooks formalize these relationships, allowing partial ordering of objectives that generalizes both Pareto and lexicographic dominance. Computing the full set of rulebook-optimal solutions, however, is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce the concept of epsilon-rule-dominance, a principled notion of approximate dominance under rulebooks, and propose RA*pex, a best-first search algorithm that efficiently computes a compact set of epsilon-approximate rulebook-optimal solutions. RA*pex leverages dimensionality reduction, a technique used to speed up existing multi-objective search algorithms, while respecting rule hierarchies by maintaining separate closed sets and performing dominance checks over truncated and residual rule sets. We provide a formal analysis of RA*pex, proving that every rulebook-optimal solution is epsilon-rule-dominated (a generalization of approximate dominance we introduce) by at least one solution in the returned set. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves computation times over two orders of magnitude faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [279] [SCOPE: Field-of-View-Aware Path Planning in Unknown 3D Environments via Safety-Volume Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04420)
*Junbin Yuan,Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE框架，用于体-mounted有限视场传感器在未知体素地图中的在线安全体积认证与规划，确保机器人运动前已观测并验证安全体积。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人安全导航与运动规划，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 机器人安全导航、运动规划、未知环境探索；传感器为体-mounted有限视场传感器；场景为3D未知环境。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构；提出SCOPE规划框架，包含认证图、乐观引导、观察义务解析、认证预览机制、轨迹优化后端。

Datasets and metrics: 60个随机化任务在三个未知3D环境中；评价指标包括任务完成率、进入非认证膨胀空间的次数、平均任务时间（预览机制减少27%）；真实机器人实验在两个代表性场景中验证。

Experiments: 在60个随机化任务中SCOPE达到所有目标且几乎零进入非认证空间；预览机制减少平均任务时间27%；真实机器人实验验证完整系统。

Abstract: Safe navigation with a body-mounted limited-field-of-view sensor requires the complete robot-inflated volume of an intended motion to be observed and verified free before execution. We formulate this requirement as online safety-volume certification in an unknown voxel map and construct a certified graph whose vertices correspond exactly to positions with fully known-free safety volumes. Based on this representation, we propose SCOPE (Safety Certification through Observation Planning and Execution), a planning framework that decouples optimistic goal-directed guidance from certified execution. SCOPE converts the first uncertified point along an optimistic route into an explicit observation obligation, resolves it through target-centric viewpoint search, and recursively clears intermediate obligations when useful viewpoints are not yet certified-reachable. A certified preview mechanism and an observation-aware trajectory optimization backend enable smooth execution. We prove conditional complete planning: under ideal monotone sensing and exhaustive finite-domain graph search, SCOPE reaches the goal whenever a finite feasible sequence of certified sensing actions exists within its planning primitives. Across 60 randomized tasks in three unknown 3D environments, SCOPE reaches every goal while maintaining near-zero entry into non-certified inflated space. Preview reduces mean mission time by 27%, and real-robot demonstrations in two representative scenarios validate the complete system.

</details>

*Yupu Lu,Shuang Wu,Sihan Chen,Ruihua Han,Yichen Zhang,Marcus Kalander,Jia Pan*

Abstract: Subtask labels decompose a long-horizon manipulation demonstration into shorter semantic segments for policy training and evaluation. Natural language descriptions are easy to read, but their linguistic variability makes automatic verification difficult. Rigid template formats, such as BEHAVIOR-1K's skill_annotation, are linguistically over-segmented, hindering both readability and annotation consistency. We propose the Structured Subtask Chain (SSC), a state-transition representation that bridges these extremes. A demonstration is a sequence of Structured Subtask Template (SST) entries. Each SST stores core action components (subject, predicate, object), flexible conditions (adverbial modifiers such as spatial or instrumental phrases), a base-motion field separate from arm actions, and an after-state scene graph. Built on this format, SSC supports three vision-language assisted functions: rendering SSTs as natural language, checking the assembled chain against four state-transition rules, and completing underspecified fields through a query resolution cascade. We instantiate the pipeline on BEHAVIOR-1K (50 tasks, 3 episodes per task, 2,357 annotated action cells) for logic verification and content completion, evaluating 13 selected state-of-the-art VL models as candidate verifiers and reporting labelling anomalies.

</details>


### [281] [GUARD: Grounding Uncertainty and Ablation-Based Risk Detection for Diffusion-Based VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04510)
*Suhas Hegde,Jitendra Yasaswi Bharadwaj Katta*

Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies can generate plausible actions even when their predictions are weakly grounded in the visual and language evidence defining the task. We introduce GUARD, a test-time failure detection method that measures this grounding without modifying the pretrained policy. GUARD estimates the influence of token-indexed entries in the final vision-language model key-value (KV) cache, constructs counterfactual caches by ablating salient KV entries, and compares their denoising responses with the original conditioning. Based on the comparison, we derive GUARD diagnostic stream including sensitivity, attention entropy, modality bias, and grounding efficiency, which are calibrated online and processed by a lightweight temporal classifier. We evaluate GUARD under task-held-out splits across five policy-benchmark settings, using Pi0, SmolVLA, and Alpamayo-1.5 on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, MetaWorld, and PhysicalAI-AV. GUARD achieves the best ROC-AUC on four of five unseen-task settings and ranks second on the remaining setting, improving the average unseen-task ROC-AUC by 5.73 percentage points over the strongest competing runtime monitor while remaining within 0.19 points of the best seen-task average. These results show that directly probing action-head dependence on multimodal evidence provides a transferable failure signal across policies, tasks, embodiments, and domains.

</details>


### [282] [Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04527)
*Yuze Fan,Yue Cao,Pengjie Gao,Haojia Gao,Guangqiu Guo,Ziyue Zhang,Junbo Tan,Bokui Chen,Zhuo Zou,Xueqian Wang*

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate temporally extended action chunks, but long-horizon manipulation remains vulnerable to accumulated execution error and visual aliasing across task stages. Successful rollouts provide useful corrective evidence, yet current frame retrieval can return progress-misaligned actions,while direct replay or time-domain fusion can overwrite the reactive structure of the policy proposal. We introduce Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency (RTCF), a training-free test-time correction framework that improves frozen VLA performance with low model-side overhead.RTCF separates which experience to retrieve from which part of its action to transfer. Progressive Memory Alignment (PMA) causally aligns the growing visual execution history with complete successful trajectories through incrementally updated monotonic frontiers, jointly identifying a relevant memory and the current aligned memory position without stage labels. From the aligned action chunk,RTCF transfers a coefficient-wise-clipped low-frequency residual on motion channels. Higher-frequency components and gripper decisions remain inherited from the frozen policy. Across four LIBERO suites and 2,000 episodes per condition, RTCF raises aggregate success from 86.4% to 88.4% and improves LIBERO-Long from 61.6% to 68.6%.These gains require no parameter updates, repeated VLA inference, or additional GPU resources: correction can be performed on the client CPU after a single policy invocation, and the median latencies sum to only 10.99 ms per action chunk

</details>

Abstract: As robotic systems become increasingly complex, generating control firmware from structural description files has emerged as a promising paradigm for reducing development complexity and improving maintainability. Existing robot description formats naturally represent robotic systems as hierarchical tree structures, where devices are recursively composed into functional subsystems and eventually into the complete robot. However, such tree-structured organization also introduces structured data dependencies that affect perception-to-decision latency and, consequently, control performance.
  In this paper, we propose FineMote, a control firmware generation framework with a scheduling mechanism tailored for tree-structured device models. The framework objectifies heterogeneous low-level control logic and exposes unified scheduling units and execution entry points. Based on the resulting object hierarchy, the scheduling mechanism exploits compile-time information to statically determine execution order with minimal runtime overhead. We prove that the proposed mechanism satisfies deadline and precedence constraints, and further derive an upper bound on intra-tree decision latency. We implement the proposed framework and evaluate it on real robotic control platforms. The experimental results show improved timing behavior and runtime responsiveness, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>

Abstract: We present GASP, a GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for real-time, collision-aware joint-space motion generation in known environments. GASP combines a clamped B-spline trajectory parameterization with a convolutional residual neural network that predicts the free interior control points, while analytically inserted boundary control points enforce initial and final derivative constraints for collision-aware planning under non-stationary conditions. A conditional variational autoencoder samples multiple trajectory candidates, which are decoded and validated in parallel on the GPU, yielding a batched planner for collision-aware coupled joint-space motion with near-millisecond inference. We validate GASP as an online motion-generation module, where it achieves analytical-level success rates with high collision-aware feasibility and substantially reduces inference time relative to GPU-based trajectory optimization. We further deploy GASP as a reinforcement-learning reset planner in competitive robotic table tennis, matching the baseline return rate while roughly halving training-time collisions.

</details>


### [285] [Mind-VLA: Instruction-Aware Spatial Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04633)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Yang Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mind-VLA，一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的指令感知空间表征对齐方法，通过目标对象的三视图特征对齐提升3D理解和操作能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于机器人操作领域的视觉-语言-动作模型研究，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等。属于泛机器学习/机器人方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作（manipulation）、视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型；应用场景：桌面操作、LIBERO和CALVIN仿真环境、真实机器人抓取任务；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：操作物体（如方块、工具等）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Mind-VLA框架：先通过语言指令定位目标对象，生成目标对象的三视图（target-object tri-view），提取VAE和VGGT特征，然后将VLA模型的隐层表征与这些特征对齐。骨干网络参数量345M。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO（成功率93.9%）、CALVIN（得分4.47）、真实机器人目标遮挡任务（平均成功率54%）。评价指标：成功率（success rate）、CALVIN得分。

Experiments: LIBERO上达到93.9%成功率，CALVIN上得分4.47，骨干网络345M参数。真实机器人目标遮挡任务平均成功率54%，比最优的指令无关方法高出32个百分点。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何遥感数据或场景；3. 不涉及轻量化技术；4. 345M参数量的模型在星上部署不现实；5. 未讨论推理速度、计算资源需求等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能参考的是其"指令感知对齐"的思想，但遥感在轨目标检测通常不需要语言指令，而是基于预定义类别进行检测。如果未来研究方向涉及遥感视觉-语言模型（如遥感图像描述、视觉问答），则可能有间接参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向不匹配。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) methods improve generalization by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods are fundamentally instruction-agnostic: the representations align the entire scene uniformly, neglecting the 3D geometry of the specific target object designated by the language instruction. This causes failures on fine-grained manipulation and target occlusion tasks, where success depends on accurate 3D understanding of the target object rather than the entire scene. To address this, we present Mind-VLA, an instruction-aware spatial representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, Mind-VLA first obtains the target object specified by the language instruction, then prepares its target-object tri-view and extracts the corresponding VAE and VGGT features. Finally, the latent representation of the VLA model is aligned with these features to enable instruction-aware 3D understanding. Mind-VLA reaches 93.9% on LIBERO and 4.47 on CALVIN with a compact 345M-parameter backbone. On real-robot tasks with target occlusion, Mind-VLA reaches 54% average success, outperforming the best-performing instruction-agnostic method in real-robot comparison by 32 percentage points. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [286] [Suppression Sticks, Locality Is Fragile: A Closed-Loop Target-and-Control Audit of Task-Vector Negation in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04692)
*Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Rushi Dai,Yiren Zhao,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Task-vector arithmetic offers a closed-form way to modify a model, yet its behavioral locality remains unclear in closed-loop robot control. We present a target-and-control audit of per-skill task-vector subtraction from multitask vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across all ten LIBERO-Goal skills, subtraction produces three qualitatively different regimes: target-control separation for five skills, resistance for three, and global collapse for two. On held-out initial states, the five suppressible targets remain at 0% success; however, mean baseline-normalized control retention is only 52%, and each target-suppressing edit materially harms at least one nominally unrelated control. Additional Goal panels show separation across tested policies with continuous-regression, discrete-token, and flow-matching action heads, whereas we observe no clean separation on Spatial and control collapse on the tested Object and Long-horizon panels. Mean task-vector cosine does not account for this variation. A matched-norm control identifies a local sign asymmetry around one Goal anchor, while multi-vector outcomes vary with anchor and scale. Retain-aware gradient baselines provide data-dependent comparators but require removal-time data and optimization; subtraction is data- and gradient-free only at edit time, assuming precomputed expert deltas. Finally, a single-skill relearning probe is consistent with behavioral masking, not certified unlearning. These results characterize task-vector subtraction as a fast but brittle intervention and underscore the need for closed-loop target-and-control evaluation when assessing locality in embodied model editing.

</details>


### [287] [Enabling Urgency-aware Robot Swarm Intralogistics using Smart IoT Tags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04721)
*Youssef Alboraei,Murray Groves,Shane Wen,Wenda Zhao,Senhui Qiu,Mohammud J. Bocus,Robert Piechocki,Sabine Hauert,Kerstin Eder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于IoT标签广播货物紧急度的去中心化机器人集群仓库物流系统，通过BLE信号实现优先级感知的搬运调度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是仓库机器人集群调度与IoT标签通信，属于室内物流/机器人领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及目标检测、遥感场景理解，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏等任何与深度学习模型压缩相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：仓库机器人集群调度（swarm logistics）；应用场景：仓库自动化、物料搬运；传感器类型：BLE（蓝牙低功耗）IoT标签；目标类别：货物/搬运载体（carrier）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。系统核心是去中心化调度策略：机器人读取BLE广播的紧急度信息，结合搬运距离进行决策。未涉及神经网络或深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文提到"ultra-low-power IoT tag"，但这是硬件层面的低功耗通信，与深度学习模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文讨论的是仓库机器人集群，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等深度学习部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真环境（simulation）和真实机器人+IoT标签载体（real robots and IoT-tagged carriers）实验。评价指标：priority alignment（优先级对齐率，即紧急货物优先搬运的比例）、P95 delivery latency（95分位交付延迟）、throughput（吞吐量）。与遥感目标检测数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 物理实验：priority alignment从0.41提升至0.64，P95延迟有非显著下降趋势，吞吐量在基线1.2%以内。仿真实验（更大规模配置）：P95延迟下降5.2%-11.8%，priority alignment提升41.7%-51.6%。无消融实验或与遥感方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何深度学习模型；3. 未讨论星上部署、边缘推理、实时性约束等；4. 仅适用于仓库场景，无法迁移到遥感领域。

Ideas for my research: 几乎无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：去中心化优先级调度策略的思想，如果类比到星上多任务调度（如不同紧急程度的观测任务优先级处理），但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（模型压缩、加速、部署）无关。

Abstract: Warehouse items differ in how urgently they must be moved: perishable goods, pharmaceutical shipments, and just-in-time production materials must be delivered sooner than the rest of the stock. Decentralised robot swarms suit warehouses that cannot justify fixed automation infrastructure, but current swarm controllers treat all items alike or rely on an external scheduler to set priorities, so urgent items wait as long as ordinary ones. This paper presents a swarm logistics system in which each warehouse carrier holds an ultra-low-power Internet-of-Things (IoT) tag that broadcasts the urgency of its item over Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE). Robots read these broadcasts directly and weigh urgency against travel distance when choosing which carrier to serve, so prioritisation happens at the item level without central scheduling. The system is evaluated in simulation and validated on real robots and IoT-tagged carriers against a proximity-only baseline. In the physical trials, priority alignment (i.e. proportion of urgent items served first), improved from 0.41 to 0.64, with a nonsignificant trend toward lower 95th-percentile (P95) delivery latency and throughput within 1.2% of the baseline. In simulation, the benefit grew with system size: across three larger configurations, P95 latency fell by 5.2% to 11.8% and priority alignment improved by 41.7% to 51.6%. Attaching urgency to the items themselves therefore allows a decentralised swarm to serve time-critical stock sooner while keeping the low infrastructure requirements that make swarm systems attractive for warehouse automation.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a fully integrated vision-based framework for real-time and robust localization, autonomous navigation, and mapping for unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs) in dynamic, visually challenging environments. The proposed pipeline enables both net-relative and global localization while generating continuous 3D maps of the surroundings in real-time. The framework was validated on synthetic datasets with ground truth and tested onboard an UUV during autonomous net-relative navigation experiments. Results demonstrate real-time performance and enhanced robustness, supporting vision-driven autonomous navigation and enabling the field deployment of marine robots for critical inspection and mapping tasks in complex underwater environments.

Abstract: Automatic train operation (ATO) at grade of automation 3 and above (GoA3-GoA4) requires robust AI-based perception systems capable of reliably detecting obstacles and railway-specific objects under real-world conditions. The effectiveness of these modern artificial intelligence approaches depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality, and highly dynamic annotated datasets. However, managing metadata, maintaining provenance, and tracking the iterative evolution of these annotations impose significant infrastructural and regulatory requirements. Existing monolithic data catalogs often suffer from massive operational overhead, poor integration into developer workflows, and severe documentation drift. This paper introduces an innovative, lightweight GitOps-based architecture for metadata management. By leveraging Data-as-Code principles, Continuous Integration/Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) pipelines, and Static Site Generation (SSG), the proposed approach establishes a seamless, developer-centric workflow. This ensures an traceability, enforces strict regulatory compliance, and automatically generates a highly performant dataset overview.

</details>


### [290] [Explicit Language Memory for Long-Horizon Planning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04765)
*Houze Xu,Jizhong Li,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层长时域VLA架构，通过显式语言记忆模块将离散时序观测转化为连贯文本记忆序列，解耦高层VLM语义推理与低层VLA连续控制，提升长时域任务的成功率与鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人长时域控制中的VLA模型，属于具身智能/机器人学领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及任何遥感场景下的目标检测或轻量化部署问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人长时域任务（long-horizon tasks）中的视觉-语言-动作联合建模。应用场景：仿真环境及真实机器人平台。传感器类型：视觉相机（RGB）。目标类别：未涉及目标检测，面向机器人操控任务。摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 分层架构：高层VLM（视觉语言模型）通过视觉问答范式进行语义推理，低层VLA执行连续控制。核心创新是显式语言记忆模块（explicit language-memory module），将离散时序观测转化为带时序逻辑的文本记忆序列，高层VLM递归更新语言记忆和子任务指令。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个仿真环境及真实机器人平台实验。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate）和鲁棒性（robustness）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在多个仿真环境和真实机器人平台上进行了sim-to-real实验，结果表明显式语言记忆提升了VLA模型在复杂长时域任务上的成功率和鲁棒性，并提供了可解释的语义决策过程。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未提供计算资源消耗数据；5. 仅在机器人操控场景验证，未在遥感/卫星场景下评估。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可考虑：1）其"显式语言记忆"思想若迁移到遥感时序分析（如多时相变化检测）中，可能有助于建模时序依赖；2）分层架构（高层语义推理+低层执行）的设计思路在星上多任务协同场景中或有启发。但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的核心方向（检测精度-速度-资源权衡）不直接相关。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified paradigm for connecting visual perception, language understanding, and robotic control. However, existing VLA models still face major challenges in long-horizon tasks: sparse expert demonstrations constrain cross-task compositional generalization; the non-Markovian nature of long-horizon tasks makes it difficult for policies conditioned only on current observations to maintain temporal consistency; limited closed-loop error correction allows execution errors to accumulate; and end-to-end action fine-tuning may weaken the high-level semantic representations of vision-language model (VLM) backbones. To address these issues, we propose a hierarchical long-horizon VLA architecture with an explicit language-memory module. The central idea is to convert discrete temporal observations into a coherent textual memory sequence with temporal logic. The system is decoupled into a high-level VLM and a low-level VLA: the high-level VLM performs semantic reasoning through a visual question answering training paradigm, while the low-level VLA executes precise continuous control conditioned on subtask instructions and visual observations. The high-level VLM recursively updates both language memory and subtask instructions using the previous memory as a contextual anchor, enabling persistent temporal tracking and dynamic correction during long-horizon execution. We evaluate the proposed method in multiple simulation environments and conduct sim-to-real experiments on a real robotic platform. The results demonstrate that explicit language memory improves the success rate and robustness of VLA models on complex long-horizon tasks while providing an interpretable semantic account of the decision process.

</details>


### [291] [From Transparent Labware Segmentation to Collision Avoidance: A Real-Time Edge-Aware Perception Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04769)
*Shijun Ding,Chen Qian,Weiwei Shang,Junlin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种边缘感知实例分割框架TransYOLO，通过轻量边缘检测分支和注意力融合实现透明实验室玻璃器皿的实时机器人避障，构建了LabGlass-IS数据集，在边界F-score和参数量上优于对比方法。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。该论文面向机器人避障场景中的透明玻璃器皿实例分割，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向无关。虽然涉及实时推理、轻量网络设计（边缘检测分支、SimAM模块）和边缘部署，但应用场景和目标（透明物体分割）与遥感目标检测差异较大。仅轻量化方法（轻量边缘分支、参数高效模块）和实时性指标（7.1ms/frame）对课题有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关，阅读优先级低。虽然轻量化和实时推理思路有间接参考价值，但场景（透明物体分割 vs 遥感目标检测）差异过大，且未涉及遥感领域核心问题（旋转框、小目标、多尺度、SAR/高光谱等）。建议仅快速浏览其轻量模块设计（SimAM、边缘分支）部分即可。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：实例分割（instance segmentation），用于机器人避障中的透明物体感知。应用场景：实验室机器人操作，透明玻璃器皿碰撞避免。传感器类型：RGB视觉（纯视觉感知）。目标类别：21类实验室玻璃器皿（烧杯、试管等）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于one-stage实时实例分割骨干网络，增强部分包括：(1) 轻量边缘检测分支（lightweight edge-detection branch）；(2) 边缘引导注意力融合（edge-guided attention fusion）；(3) 无参数SimAM模块（parameter-free SimAM module）。整体框架称为TransYOLO，结合多视角掩码质心三角测量实现3D定位。

Lightweight method: 轻量化体现在：(1) 轻量边缘检测分支（具体结构摘要未说明）；(2) 无参数SimAM注意力模块（parameter-free，不增加额外可学习参数）；(3) 整体模型参数量仅为最接近精度对比方法的2.85%。但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 推理速度：7.1ms/frame（约140 FPS），满足实时性要求。参数量：仅为最接近精度对比方法的2.85%（具体数值摘要未说明）。FLOPs、显存、能耗、具体参数量数值摘要未说明。部署场景为机器人边缘计算，非星上部署，但实时性和轻量特性对边缘部署有参考价值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LabGlass-IS，自建数据集，包含3485张图像，21个类别，真实实验室玻璃器皿实例分割标注。评价指标：Boundary F-score（边界F分数，最高97.80），推理速度（7.1ms/frame），参数量（对比方法2.85%），碰撞避免成功率（93.3%）。未使用遥感领域常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 主要对比方法：YOLO-prompted FastSAM框架。结果：所提方法在Boundary F-score上达到97.80，比FastSAM高18.93 BF点；参数量仅为最接近精度对比方法的2.85%；推理速度7.1ms/frame。真实机器人实验碰撞避免成功率93.3%。消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星场景完全无关，不涉及遥感图像特性（多尺度、旋转变化、背景复杂、小目标等）；(2) 仅针对透明物体边界分割，泛化到遥感目标检测需大量改造；(3) 轻量化方法较为简单（仅边缘分支+SimAM），未涉及量化、剪枝、蒸馏等更深入的压缩技术；(4) 参数量和FLOPs具体数值未给出，难以评估其轻量化程度；(5) 未在遥感数据集上验证。

Ideas for my research: (1) 边缘引导注意力融合（edge-guided attention fusion）的思路可借鉴到遥感小目标检测中——遥感图像中目标边界信息对检测也有帮助，可尝试将边缘检测分支引入轻量遥感检测模型；(2) 无参数SimAM模块的设计思路（parameter-free attention）可用于降低遥感模型的额外参数开销；(3) 该工作证明了在保持高精度的同时将参数量压缩到对比方法的2.85%是可行的，这种极端压缩比例对星上部署有启发；(4) 实时推理速度7.1ms/frame（约140 FPS）可作为星上实时检测的参考指标。但需注意场景差异大，直接迁移需重新设计。

Abstract: This paper presents an edge-aware instance segmentation framework that enables real-time robotic collision avoidance with transparent laboratory glassware using purely visual perception. Transparent vessels defy conventional segmentation due to refraction, specular reflection, and the absence of stable interior texture, yet their boundary contours remain comparatively reliable visual cues. Exploiting this observation, we augment a one-stage real-time instance segmentation backbone with a lightweight edge-detection branch, edge-guided attention fusion, and a parameter-free SimAM module, and further construct LabGlass-IS, a 3485-image, 21-category instance segmentation dataset of real laboratory glassware. The enhanced model achieves the highest Boundary F-score of 97.80 among compared methods, outperforming the YOLO-prompted FastSAM framework by 18.93 BF points. Furthermore, it maintains an inference speed of 7.1ms per frame and requires only 2.85% of the parameters of the closest accuracy competitor. Multi-view triangulation of mask centroids further provides 3D positions for conservative bounding-volume collision constraints. Real-robot trials achieve a 93.3% collision avoidance success rate, indicating the feasibility of the proposed perception-to-action pipeline for robot collision avoidance among fragile transparent objects. Our code is available at https://github.com/havishamy/TransYOLO_3D. Our video is available at https://havishamy.github.io/paper-videos/.

</details>


### [292] [Deliberate Before You Fly: Vision-Guided Spatial Deliberation for UAV See-and-Reach Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04825)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Bohang Liu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: UAV see-and-reach navigation requires an aerial agent to approach a language-specified target visible in its initial view and stop reliably near it. Existing methods typically map vision-language representations directly to action outputs without explicitly modeling intermediate fine-grained spatial decisions. This direct mapping causes semantic-control misalignment, leading to inconsistent maneuvers and unreliable termination. To address this issue, we propose DBFly, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework that introduces explicit vision-guided spatial deliberation before waypoint generation. Specifically, DBFly introduces a spatial maneuver decision chain that progressively performs target-direction anchoring, spatial diagnosis, and maneuver decision, enabling high-level maneuver intent to explicitly guide continuous waypoint generation. DBFly further constructs an implicit flight corridor by transforming the initial target-direction prior into a persistent geometric reference and deriving an online corridor state from the UAV's current position, thereby providing soft geometric guidance for spatial diagnosis and maneuver correction. In addition, DBFly develops a terminal-convergence-aware stopping strategy that characterizes terminal states through both target proximity and short-horizon motion convergence, enabling more reliable stopping near the target. Extensive experiments across seen, unseen-object, and unseen-scene test sets demonstrate that DBFly improves the success rate over the SOTA baseline by an average of 25.07 percentage points. The project homepage is available at https://xuefanfu.github.io/DBFly-Page.

</details>


### [293] [RORA: Realistic Object Reconstruction with Articulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04842)
*Hyesung Lee,Youngseon Lee,Kyutae Lee,Dongjun Lee,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个从单段静态物体视频通过人机协作重建带准确关节结构的仿真资产（3DGS+网格混合表示）的端到端管线，用于机器人学习中的仿真环境构建。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人学习中的真实-仿真环境重建（NeRF/3DGS），核心任务是物体关节结构重建与仿真资产导出，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨处理、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：仿真资产重建（articulated asset reconstruction），场景：机器人学习中的真实-仿真迁移（sim-to-real），传感器类型：单目静态视频（RGB），目标类别：日常物体（如PartNet-Mobility数据集中的铰接物体）。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 混合表示：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 用于照片级渲染 + 基于网格的几何用于物理交互。管线包括凸分解、用户分组、3D高斯绑定到网格部件、自动关节建议算法（Automatic Joint Suggestion Algorithm）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提及在Unreal Engine和NVIDIA Isaac Sim中部署用于实时灵巧手操作，但这是仿真环境部署，非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PartNet-Mobility-v0 数据集 + 真实物体。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体量化指标，仅提及"precise articulation results"。

Experiments: 在PartNet-Mobility-v0数据集和真实物体上验证了关节重建精度。展示了在Unreal Engine和NVIDIA Isaac Sim中的机器人学习应用（实时灵巧手操作）。

Limitations: 1. 需要人机协作（human-in-the-loop），自动化程度有限；2. 仅针对铰接物体（articulated objects），非通用场景重建；3. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；4. 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论。

Ideas for my research: 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的点是3DGS的渲染效率，但该论文本身不涉及轻量化，且应用场景（机器人仿真）与遥感在轨推理完全不同。

Abstract: Replicating real-world environments into simulation by realistic visual representation like NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an effective strategy to reduce the sim-to-real gap in robot learning. However, implementing object articulation during the real-to-sim process is still a challenging task. Existing motion tracking or learning based articulation methods shows low success rates on complex kinematic structures having multiple joints. Furthermore, those methods require scan of dynamic motion of objects, which makes reconstruction process much complicated. In this work, we propose the first end-to-end pipeline that reconstructs simulation-ready assets with accurate articulation from a single static object video input through suggestion based human-in-the-loop process. Our approach exports a hybrid representation combining 3DGS for photorealistic rendering and mesh-based geometry for physical interaction. In the reconstruction process, our pipeline performs convex decomposition followed by user grouping for intuitive part segmentation, subsequently binding 3D Gaussians to the corresponding mesh parts. An Automatic Joint Suggestion Algorithm then calculates candidate joint axes from local boundary geometries and presents them to users for efficient articulated asset reconstruction. We have shown that our method achieves precise articulation results on partnet-mobility-v0 dataset and real objects. Additionally we presented a potential usage of our framework on robot learning, deploying the reconstructed assets in Unreal Engine and NVIDIA Isaac Sim, demonstrating real-time dexterous hand manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [294] [PRIMAL3: Pathfinding via Reinforcement and Imitation Multi-Agent Learning - Leveraging LaCAM3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04905)
*Chengyang He,Tanishq Duhan,Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Fangyuan Wang,Yuhong Cao,Jiankai Sun,Ge Sun,Mac Schwager,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: We present PRIMAL3, an ultra-large-scale learning-based framework for multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) that integrates reinforcement learning, topology-aware communication, LaCAM3-guided training, and PIBT-based action refinement. PRIMAL3 targets failures at topologically critical states, where agents must coordinate decisively around bottlenecks, dead ends, and persistent conflicts. Each agent is represented using features derived from cut vertices, dead-end regions, shortest-path distances, and blocking estimates. Two complementary graphs capture agent interactions: a same-direction following graph propagates multihop context along compatible paths, while a different-direction conflict graph differentiates agents competing for shared space through masked attention and relative features. During training, we propose to let policy entropy identify uncertain agents, for which LaCAM3 provides confidence-triggered action interventions and label-smoothed imitation targets. During execution, a priority-aware PIBT module refines the proposed joint actions using persistent, learned, and distance-aware priorities together with policy-aware fallback preferences while maintaining collision-free execution. The resulting framework combines learned exploration with structured expert guidance without requiring LaCAM3 at inference. Experiments demonstrate that PRIMAL3 substantially outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based baselines and scales to ultra-large instances with up to city-level 100,000 agents. Real-world experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of deploying PRIMAL3 on physical robotic systems and ablation studies validate the individual contributions the components we proposed. Project page: https://marmotlab.github.io/PRIMAL3/

</details>


### [295] [Mimir: A Neuro-Symbolic Memory System with Dynamic Grounding for Embodied Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04933)
*Haoming Xu,Zhenlin He,Hengyi Wang,Jiafeng Xu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mimir神经符号记忆模块，将世界记忆与任务记忆分离并动态关联，用于长时域具身任务中的场景信念维护与执行进度追踪。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是具身智能（embodied AI）中的长时域任务记忆与规划问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长时域具身任务（long-horizon embodied task）；应用场景：室内具身导航与操作（EB-ALFRED, EB-Habitat）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（推测为RGB-D等室内传感器）；目标类别：物体位置、物体状态、手部状态等具身任务元素。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Mimir神经符号记忆架构，包含：世界记忆（World memory，维护物体位置、状态、感知证据）、任务记忆（Task memory，维护目标议程、进度状态、手部状态、失败记录、执行约束）、接地模块（Grounding module，将活跃目标关联到世界候选、填充缺失源位置、附加证据）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EB-ALFRED、EB-Habitat（含Long-horizon子集）；评价指标：成功率（Success Rate）。

Experiments: 在多个backbone上测试，Mimir在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat任务上最大提升42.5%，平均提升23.0%；与同backbone下最优基线相比，平均成功率提升8.5%；在EB-Habitat Long-horizon子集上达到86.0%成功率，超越当前闭源模型。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等工程部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的神经符号记忆分离思想（世界记忆与任务记忆解耦）在理论上可启发遥感在轨场景中"场景记忆"与"检测任务记忆"的分离设计，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化目标检测模型）关联极弱。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied task requires agents to act under partial observability while preserving both scene belief and execution progress. Flat histories or implicit policy states may contain past observations, but they do not provide an explicit interface for deciding which world facts support the currently active goal. We introduce Mimir, a neuro-symbolic memory that separates world memory from task memory and dynamically grounds them before each action. World memory maintains object locations, object states, and perceptual evidence, while task memory maintains an ordered goal agenda, progress state, hand state, failures, and execution constraints. A grounding module binds the active goal to recalled world candidates, fills missing source locations, and attaches evidence before planning and embodiment-specific execution. Across tested backbones, Mimir consistently improves on different EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat tasks, with maximum gains of 42.5% and average gains of 23.0%, respectively. Compared with the best results among prior agent and memory systems evaluated under the same backbone, Mimir improves the overall average success rate by 8.5%. Finally, on the EB-Habitat Long-horizon subset, Mimir achieves 86.0% success rate, substantially outperforming current closed-source models. Our code will be released soon.

</details>


### [296] [DreamWAM: Beyond RGB Future Prediction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04996)
*Shanglin Yuan,Weiheng Zhao,Xin Shi,Haoyi Jiang,Xianda Guo,Liu Liu,Wenyu Liu,Wei Sui,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) learn action-relevant representations by predicting how the observed world will evolve. Most existing WAMs define this future in RGB space, where task-relevant state transitions are entangled with nuisance variations in texture, illumination, background, and viewpoint. We argue that WAMs should explicitly predict action-relevant future state rather than relying on RGB prediction alone. We introduce DreamWAM, which reformulates future prediction as structured world modeling beyond RGB, representing future states through complementary views of appearance, motion, geometry, and semantics. During training, DreamWAM combines joint latent denoising of RGB and motion with lightweight gated residual branches for geometry and semantics. Shared attention between VideoDiT and ActionDiT allows the action branch to learn from these future-state predictions, while all beyond-RGB supervision branches are disabled at inference and deployment remains RGB-only. Across both no-rollout and joint video-action inference, DreamWAM consistently improves the matched RGB-only baselines on LIBERO, from 97.30\% to 98.40\% and from 98.00\% to 98.90\%, respectively. The gains become larger under unseen LIBERO-Plus perturbations, from 51.36\% to 63.44\% and from 69.16\% to 75.47\%. The same robustness extends to real-world manipulation, where DreamWAM attains an average success rate of 74.4\% across unseen changes in lighting, background, and object layout, compared with 55.6\% for Fast-WAM-Joint. These results show that robust world-action learning depends not only on predicting the future, but on representing it in a form that matters for action. The code and models are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/DreamWAM.

</details>

Abstract: We study the synthesis of optimal policies for planning problems on Markov decision processes with both objectives and safety constraints specified in co-safe linear temporal logic (sc-LTL). Our problems are inherently non-Markovian due to the complexity of the sc-LTL specification and may require policy randomization to balance the objective and constraint. We propose a novel approach that reduces the constrained sc-LTL planning problem to a constrained reachability problem on an extended model. We then show that a class of switching policies constructed from stationary policies for the individual sc-LTL specifications is sufficient for optimality for the constrained reachability problem. Our finding enables a tractable linear program to compute the optimal policy. A grid world case study demonstrates that our switching policies can achieve the optimal trade-off between the objective and the safety constraint and validates both optimality and tractability.

</details>


### [298] [BridgeVLA++: A Data-Efficient, Generalizable, and Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Framework for 3D Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05042)
*Peiyan Li,Yuze Zhu,Yixiang Chen,Qisen Ma,Yuan Xu,Jiabing Yang,He Guan,Yan Huang,Hongtao Wu,Xiao Ma,Tao Kong,Liang Wang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BridgeVLA++，一种面向3D机器人操作的记忆增强型视觉-语言-动作模型，通过统一时空记忆架构提升数据效率、泛化能力和记忆感知操作能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于3D机器人操作（robot manipulation），属于具身智能/机器人领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心概念，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D机器人操作（3D robot manipulation），包括空间操作（spatial manipulation）和记忆依赖操作（memory-dependent manipulation）。应用场景：机器人抓取、操作、双机械臂协作。传感器类型：3D点云（point clouds）和多视图图像。目标类别：机器人操作对象，非遥感目标。摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）构建视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。核心架构：BridgeVLA++在BridgeVLA基础上增加统一时空记忆架构（unified spatio-temporal memory architecture），建模持久空间上下文（persistent spatial context）和时间交互历史（temporal interaction history）。输入：原始点云投影为多视图图像，预测中间热图（intermediate heatmaps）后生成机器人动作。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向机器人平台，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。提及在空间操作任务上表现良好，在两个具有挑战性的记忆依赖操作基准上达到SOTA，并在真实机器人平台上验证。

Experiments: 实验表明：在空间操作任务上表现良好且泛化鲁棒；在两个记忆依赖操作基准上达到SOTA，且不牺牲BridgeVLA的数据效率和泛化能力；在双机械臂设置和真实机器人平台上有效，证明跨任务、环境和机器人平台的可扩展性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算资源需求、推理速度等关键部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：(1) 预训练VLM的输入-输出对齐保持策略（preserving input-output alignment）——在遥感轻量化中可考虑如何保持预训练知识的迁移效率；(2) 时空记忆架构用于时序遥感数据（如视频卫星、时序SAR）的上下文建模思路；(3) 多视图投影策略（点云→多视图图像）可类比遥感多视角融合。但整体方向差异大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Leveraging pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) to construct vision-language-action (VLA) models has emerged as a promising paradigm for 3D robot manipulation. However, existing 3D VLA methods remain data-hungry, exhibit limited generalization under distribution shifts, and lack explicit memory of past observations. These limitations hinder their application to data-scarce, open-world, and memory-dependent manipulation scenarios. Our previous work, BridgeVLA, improves data efficiency and generalization by preserving the input--output alignment of a pre-trained VLM during 3D action learning: raw point clouds are projected into multi-view images, and intermediate heatmaps are predicted before generating robot actions. In this work, we develop BridgeVLA++ by equipping BridgeVLA with a unified spatio-temporal memory architecture that models persistent spatial context and temporal interaction history. The resulting memory-augmented framework can reason over observation histories while preserving BridgeVLA's data efficiency and generalization capabilities. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves strong performance on spatial manipulation tasks while exhibiting robust generalization. BridgeVLA++ further achieves state-of-the-art performance on two challenging memory-dependent manipulation benchmarks without sacrificing the data efficiency and generalization of the original BridgeVLA. In addition, BridgeVLA++ performs effectively in bimanual manipulation settings and is validated on an additional real-world robotic platform, demonstrating its scalability across tasks, environments, and robotic platforms. These results establish BridgeVLA++ as a unified 3D vision-language-action framework that simultaneously supports data-efficient learning, robust generalization, and effective memory-aware robot manipulation. Project website: https://bridgevla-plus.github.io/.

</details>


### [299] [SpikingNav: Robust Embodied Navigation with Spiking Neural Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05078)
*Jiahong Zhang,Sijun Shen,Dehua Wu,Yifan Lin,Xuechen Xia,Xu Chu,Youhui Zhang,GuoqiLi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpikingNav，一种基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的室内具身导航框架，利用脉冲编码感知和策略动力学提升鲁棒性，并在神经形态芯片上验证部署可行性。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及轻量化和边缘部署（SNN、神经形态芯片），但场景是室内具身导航（PointNav/ObjectNav），与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关。目标检测也不是本文任务。仅SNN的轻量化和芯片部署思路对星上部署有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关，仅SNN轻量化和芯片部署思路有间接参考价值，但场景和任务完全不匹配，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：室内具身导航（PointNav, ObjectNav）；场景：室内环境；传感器：egocentric视觉观测；目标类别：导航目标（物体/点）；与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: SpikingNav包含两个子网络：Spiking Sensing Encoder (SSE)——基于脉冲的视觉特征提取骨干；Spiking Policy Network (SPN)——通过膜积分、阈值发放和脉冲触发重置维持循环策略状态的脉冲策略网络。整体采用SNN架构，事件驱动计算。

Lightweight method: 采用脉冲神经网络(SNN)作为轻量化手段。SNN通过事件驱动计算（spike-based computation）实现低功耗，参数量少于匹配的ANN基线，每步计算量更低。在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上进行了物理部署验证。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗数值。但提到"fewer parameters and lower per-step computation than a matched ANN baseline"，并在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上验证了部署可行性。对星上部署的参考价值在于SNN的低功耗特性适合资源受限平台。

Datasets and metrics: 评估任务：PointNav（点导航）和ObjectNav（物体导航）；评估指标：Success（成功率），包括clean条件下的成功率和visual corruptions下的平均成功率；数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 与匹配的ANN基线对比：ObjectNav成功率从31.05%提升至34.12%（clean）；视觉损坏下平均成功率从8.45%提升至13.71%。在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上验证了脉冲感知方法的可部署性。

Limitations: 1. 场景为室内导航，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测场景差异巨大；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化部署指标的具体数值；4. 未与遥感领域常用的轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）对比；5. 仅在单一神经形态芯片上验证，未讨论星上环境（辐射、温度、功耗预算）的适配性。

Ideas for my research: 1. SNN用于遥感在轨目标检测的可行性探索：SNN的事件驱动特性理论上适合星上低功耗场景，可尝试将遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列）的骨干替换为脉冲神经元；2. 神经形态芯片部署经验：Thruster-V2的部署流程可参考，但需评估星上可用神经形态芯片（如Loihi、Speck）的适配性；3. 鲁棒性思路：SNN对视觉损坏的鲁棒性提升思路可迁移到遥感图像噪声（如云雾遮挡、传感器噪声）场景；4. 需注意：本文是导航任务而非检测任务，检测头部的脉冲化设计仍需自行探索。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires an agent to make sequential decisions from egocentric observations in a physical environment. Existing Artificial Neural Network (ANN)-based navigation models have achieved strong performance, yet they often rely on dense computation and may degrade under visual corruptions. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide event-driven computation and intrinsic temporal dynamics, which are promising for compact and robust navigation on resource-constrained platforms. However, whether spike-based sensing and policy dynamics can improve robustness in visually rich embodied navigation remains an open problem. This paper proposes SpikingNav, a spiking framework for robust indoor embodied navigation. It contains a Spiking Sensing Encoder (SSE) and a Spiking Policy Network (SPN). The SSE extracts task-conditioned visual features with a spike-based backbone. The SPN maintains a recurrent policy state through membrane integration, thresholding, and spike-triggered reset. In this way, SpikingNav exploits the dynamic properties and spike activations of SNNs to improve navigation performance and robustness. We evaluate SpikingNav on PointNav and ObjectNav under clean observations and visual corruptions. SpikingNav achieves competitive clean performance and stronger robustness with fewer parameters and lower per-step computation than a matched ANN baseline. For instance, SpikingNav improves ObjectNav success from 31.05% to 34.12%, and raises the average success under visual corruptions from 8.45% to 13.71%, demonstrating the benefits of spike-based sensing and policy dynamics. We further validate the deployability of our spike-based sensing method on the Thruster-V2 neuromorphic chip. This physical hardware validation shows that SpikingNav can be instantiated on a real neuromorphic substrate for cyber-physical systems.

</details>
